                                                             RTTS                                 مگنست یا سیمان تزریق برای

باید رمز و هایدریل بین فشار ترخیص برای اما ؛ندیممیب را رمز ،کند پیدا نشتی و باشد شده بسته هایدریل اگر خوانی فشار درهنگام   4. - 7

VACUUM.OPEN WELL.POH TO SURF, WHILE PUMP 6 BPM WATER IN ANN.RIH W/7" RTTS FOLLOWED BY 3-1/2" & 5" DPS TO 985M
WHILE PUMP 6.5 BPM WATER IN ANN.R/UP CMT LINE & TEST W/3000 PSI,OK.SET 7" RTTS @ 985M & TEST W/1000PSI,OK.MIX
MAGNESET AS PROG WHILE CHECK ANN,STATIC. MONITOR PRESS IN DPS, AFTER 3HRS PRESS 800PSI.BULL HEAD WELL W/100BBL
WATER PUMP 120 BBL LCM PILL(35#).PUMP 150 BBL 100 PCF MAGNESET.DISP W/80 BBL WATER AS LEVEL OFF.(OBS NO PRESS.WHILE
PUMP & DISPLACE MAGNESET) WAIT ON MAGNESET WHILE MONITOR PRESS,AFTER 3 HRS, PRESS 800 PSI
……….
CONT CLOSE WELL & WAIT ON MAG WHILE MONITOR PRESS AFTER 16 HRS ZERO, OPEN WELL F/CHOKE, NO FLUID, PRIME WELL “ OBS
WELL FULL", UNSET RTTS PKR, CIRC MUD BTM'S UP W/NL-NG, POOH TO SURF, CHECK & L/D 7" RTTS, OK NL-NG

                                                                                                    . اریم دیم نگه سیلیپس برای مقداری و ،هیمدیم ن قرار S.NPL روی را ا هه لول وزن یه هم   5.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           : هدف   6.
قبل محاسبات از بیش ده هبهر سازند یا و گچساران الیه فشار( باالتر COLLAPSE و BURST مقاومت  با برداری هبهر جداری راندن   1. - 6
                                                                                                                                                                           ). است
                                                             ده بهره سازند سیال توسط جداری گی هشد خورده از جلوگیری   2. - 6
                                                                                       مراحل
                                                                        بوش پایین و باال دادن جوش   1.
                                                                                                     اسپول دادن قرار   2.
                                                                  ) اسپول باالی از نشتی بررسی( پایین فضای آزمایش و پایین P.SEAL کردن پک   3.
1.RUN IN WITH THE CASING WITH SHOE TRACK CONFIGURED AS FOLLOWS:
TIE-BACK STEM
ONE FULL JOINT OF CASING
ORIFICE FLOAT COLLAR (WITHOUT BACK PRESSURE DEVICE) OR BAFFLE COLLAR.
2.TAG THE TOP OF THE TIE-BACK EXTENSION OR PBR BY INSTALLING A CIRCULATING HEAD WHEN APPROACHING IT AND
CIRCULATING SLOWLY WHILE OBSERVING FOR A PRESSURE BUILD-UP.AS SOON AS THE PRESSURE BUILD UP OCCURS, SHUT OFF THE
PUMPS.THE PRESSURE SHALL ONLY BE ALLOWED TO BUILD UP TO A MAXIMUM VALUE 250 PSI.
3.BOTTOM OUT THE STRING BY APPLYING 1 000 LBS WEIGHT DOWN.PRESSURE TEST THE CASING AND TIE-BACK SEALS TO 3 000 PSI,
BLEED OFF PRESSURE.
4.PULL BACK, SPACE OUT THE CASING, AND INSTALL THE HANGER ASSEMBLY.MAINTAIN ACCURACY WITHIN 0.3 M WHILE SPACED
OUT TO THE 1 000 LBS WEIGHT DOWN POSITION.
NOTE: IN CASE THE STRING IS NOT TO BE CEMENTED, THE SPACE OUT WILL BE ADVISED.
5.LOWER CASING AGAIN WHILE PUMPING.AFTER A PRESSURE BUILD UP OCCURS (INDICATING THAT THE FIRST SEAL HAS ENTERED
THE SEAL BORE) CHECK THE SPACE-OUT AND CORRECT IF NECESSARY.BOTTOM OUT TO DOUBLE CHECK THE SPACE OUT.
6.PULL THE STRING ABOVE THE TIE-BACK POSITION.
7.SLOWLY LOWER STRING WHILE A DECREASE IN STRING WEIGHT WILL INDICATE THAT THE STEM SEALS ARE ENTERING THE TIE-
BACK EXTENSION OR PBR.BLEED OFF PRESSURE AND OPEN CASING TO ATMOSPHERE WHILE LOWERING THE STRING UNTIL THE
CASING IS LANDED.

1400 شهریور :ویرایش آخرین                          102

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                                                     سرتاسری الینر

8.CIRCULATE THE CASING CONTENTS + 25 % AND CEMENT AS PER THE CEMENTING PROGRAM.TEST CASING AS REQUIRED
IN PRESSURE TESTING -TIE-BACK PACKERS OF THIS CHAPTER AFTER BUMPING THE PLUG.
9.BLEED OFF TEST PRESSURE.HOLD ENOUGH PRESSURE TO CONTAIN THE HYDROSTATIC DIFFERENTIAL PRESSURE FROM THE
ANNULUS.
10.IN THE EVENT OF A STEM SEAL LEAK, TO AVOID BACK FLOW, CLOSE CASING WHILE WATCHING FOR A PRESSURE BUILD-UP IN THE
STRING.IF NO BUILD-UP OF PRESSURE OCCURS, BACK OFF LANDING STRING OR INSTALL EMERGENCY HANGER ASSEMBLY.IF BUILD-
UP OCCURS, DELAY RIGGING DOWN UNTIL THE CEMENT IS HARD.

             )(میل بدون نوع WINDOW کردن باز   9.
SET THE BRIDGE PLUG, AS CLOSE AS POSSIBLE TO THE DEPTH, WHERE THE WINDOW HAS TO BE MILLED   . 10
                                                                                                                                                                          . TO ACTIVATE THE SETTING ANCHOR
SPACE OUT THE STRING: 3-6 MT OF THE KELLY IS ABOVE THE TABLE ONCE THE WHIPSTOCK IS SET,   . 11
OTHERWISE THE WINDOW MILL CANNOT BE RETRACTED FROM THE WHIPSTOCK ASSEMBLY DUE TO THE
PRESENCE OF THE KICK-OFF PAD (THE 8IN WATER MELON MILL, POSITIONED ON TOP OF THE WINDOW
                                                                                                         . MILL, HAS TO MILL THIS PAD FIRST TO GIVE A 8IN CLEARANCE)
NOTE: ONCE THE WHIPSTOCK IS SET, THE KELLY CAN’T BE RAISED, UNLESS THE WHIPSTOCK IS PULLED UP   . 12
AS WELL (IF STILL POSSIBLE) WHICH COULD HAVE SERIOUS WELL CONTROL IMPLICATIONS.FOR THIS
REASON A CBL IS REQUIRED AND MILLING SHOULD ONLY TAKE PLACE IF HYDROCARBON BEARING ZONES
                                                                     ARE ISOLATED
                                                             NOTES
                                                                                                                              . UBHO SHOULD BE ALIGNED TO THE WHIPSTOCK FACE   1.
                                                                                                                                                                                                      . MILLS SHALL BE GAUGED   2.
                               . SHEAR BOLT SHALL BE SECURED WITH A LOCK RING AND INSTALLED IN DOWNSHEAR MODE   3.
                                                                                                                       . ENSURE ‘BIT’ BREAKER IS AVAILABLE FOR WINDOW MILL   4.
ASSEMBLE AND TORQUE UP MILLING ASSEMBLY PRIOR TO PICKING UP WHIPSTOCK ASSEMBLY AND   5.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               . ANCHOR
                                            RUNNING AND ORIENTATING WHIPSTOCK   6.
WHEN RUNNING AND ORIENTATION THE WHIPSTOCK THE FOLLOWING PROCEDURE SHOULD BE   7.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        : FOLLOWED
8.  1.RIH THE WHIPSTOCK ASSEMBLY TO ONE JOINT ABOVE THE BRIDGE PLUG.
9.  2.SPACE-OUT THE STRING.
10. 3.RUN GYRO WHILE WORKING TORQUE OUT OF THE STRING.
11. 4.ORIENTATE WHIPSTOCK FACE TO THE REQUIRED AZIMUTH ±30 DEG.
12. 5.POOH GYRO.
NOTES:
13. RIH AT CONTROLLED SPEED AND SET SLIPS GENTLY TO AVOID WEAKENING SHEAR BOLT.
14. USE DRILLPIPE WIPER WHILE RIH.
15. CHECK UBHO DEPTH, IN RELATION TO PIPE TALLY, WHILE RUNNING THE GYRO WIRE LINE.
16. SETTING WHIPSTOCK
TO SET THE WHIPSTOCK:
17. 1.TAG BRIDGE PLUG WITH THE KELLY INSTALLED WITH 4-5 KLBS AND CHECK SPACEOUT, (3-6 MT OF KELLY
   ABOVE THE TABLE).
18. 2.PULL BACK 1 MT AND CIRCULATE TO MILLING/DRILLING MUD IF REQUIRED.

1400 شهریور :ویرایش آخرین                           186

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                                       WHIP STOCK استاک ویپ

19. 3.TAKE UP AND DOWN READINGS WITH AND WITHOUT PUMPS ON.
20. 4.LOWER THE STRING AND ACTIVATE THE ANCHOR, BY APPLYING WEIGHT ON TOP BRIDGE PLUG, THE
   SHEAR PIN SHOULD SHEAR WITH 10 KLBS.
21. 5.SET THE ANCHOR WITH 15 - 18 KLBS DOWN WEIGHT.
22. 6.CHECK ANCHOR SET BY PULLING 5 KLBS ABOVE PICK-UP WEIGHT.
23. 7.SHEAR THE WINDOW MILL FROM THE WHIPSTOCK IN STAGES OF 10 KLBS UNTIL THE BOLT SHEARS 35±
    KLBS.
   MILLING WINDOW:
1.  1.BREAK CIRCULATION AND PUMP AT 125 GPM.
2.  2.PICK-UP TO NEUTRAL AND ATTEMPT TO ROTATE STRING AT 20 RPM, RECORD THE REQUIRED TORQUE.
3.  3.SLOWLY INCREASE PUMP RATE TO 210 GPM AND INCREASE ROTARY SPEED TO 45 RPM WHILE
   MAINTAINING NEUTRAL WEIGHT.
4.  4.MAKE INITIAL CUT WITH 1 - 3 KLBS WOB, MAINTAINING THESE PARAMETERS FOR AT LEAST 3 FT.
5.  5.INCREASE PARAMETERS GRADUALLY TO 80 TO 100 RPM, 4 -10 KLBS WOB AND PUMP RATE ACCORDING
   TO MUD PARAMETERS AND RETURNS SEEN ON SURFACE.
6.  NOTE: AS A RULE OF THUMB FOR MILLING CASING IN GENERAL, WHEN MILLING WELL CEMENTED
   CASING, THE PENETRATION RATE CAN BE INCREASED BY USING HIGHER WEIGHT AND ROTARY
   SPEED.CONVERSELY, WHEN MILLING BADLY CEMENTED CASING, LOW WEIGHT AND ROTARY SPEED
   SHOULD BE USED.
7.  6.MILL AND DRILL POCKET SUFFICIENT ENOUGH TO ENSURE THAT THE FULL SIZE WATERMELON MILL HAS
   EXITED THE WINDOW.
8.  7.BACKREAM GENTLY TO STARTING POSITION AND REAM WINDOW ONCE MORE.
9.  8.CHECK WINDOW CLEAR BY LOWERING THE MILLS THROUGH IT WITHOUT ROTATING.IF MORE THAN 3
   KLBS EXCESS DRAG IS OBSERVED REAM THE WINDOW ONCE AGAIN (EXCESSIVE REAMING WILL LEAD TO
   EXCESSIVE WEAR ON THE WHIPSTOCK SLIDE).
10. 9.SWEEP HOLE AND POOH.
11. 10.RE-MEASURE THE DIAMETER OF THE UPPER WATERMELON MILL WHEN THE MILLING ASSEMBLY IS ON
    SURFACE.IF THE MILL IS MORE THAN 1/10IN UNDERGAUGE A REAMING TRIP WITH A FULL GAUGE MILL
   SHOULD BE MADE.
